← HERE\\n    noisy_action_projector=None,\\n    use_film: bool = False,\\n    **kwargs: str,\\n) -> np.ndarray:\\n```\\n\\n## 2) How does it use the projector for vision tokens?\\n\\nThe vision-to-LLM projector is used in the `_process_vision_features` method to project patch embeddings into the language embedding space:\\n\\n```python\\ndef _process_vision_features(self, pixel_values, language_embeddings=None, use_film=False):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Process vision features with optional FiLM conditioning\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    \\n    if use_film:\\n        patch_features = self.vision_backbone(pixel_values, language_embeddings)\\n    else:\\n        patch_features = self.vision_backbone(pixel_values)\\n    \\n    # Project patch embeddings into language embedding space\\n    return self.projector(patch_features)  # ← Applies projector\\n```\\n\\nWithin `predict_action`, it's called as:\\n\\n```python\\nprojected_patch_embeddings = self._process_vision_features(pixel_values, language_embeddings, use_film)\\n```\\n\\n## 3) Attribute name for the vision-to-LLM projector\\n\\n**The attribute name is `self.projector`** (defined in `PrismaticForConditionalGeneration.__init__`):\\n\\n```python\\n# Create Multimodal Projector\\nself.projector = PrismaticProjector(\\n    config.use_fused_vision_backbone,\\n    vision_dim=self.vision_backbone.embed_dim,\\n    llm_dim=config.text_config.hidden_size,\\n)\\n```\\n\\nThe `PrismaticProjector` class:\\n\\n```python\\nclass PrismaticProjector(nn.Module):\\n    def __init__(self, use_fused_vision_backbone: bool, vision_dim: int, llm_dim: int) -> None:\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.use_fused_vision_backbone = use_fused_vision_backbone\\n        self.vision_dim, self.llm_dim = vision_dim, llm_dim\\n        \\n        if not self.use_fused_vision_backbone:\\n            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(self.vision_dim, self.llm_dim, bias=True)\\n            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(self.llm_dim, self.llm_dim, bias=True)\\n            self.act_fn1 = nn.GELU()\\n```\\n\\n## 4) How does it handle the action head for L1 regression?\\n\\nThe `action_head` is used in the `_regression_or_discrete_prediction` method for **L1 regression-based continuous action prediction**:\\n\\n```python\\ndef _regression_or_discrete_prediction(\\n    self,\\n    input_embeddings,\\n    all_actions_mask,\\n    projected_patch_embeddings,\\n    attention_mask,\\n    labels,\\n    NUM_PATCHES,\\n    NUM_PROMPT_TOKENS,\\n    action_head=None,  # ← Optional parameter\\n):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Run L1 regression-based continuous action prediction or discrete action tokens prediction.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    \\n    # ... (build multimodal embeddings and run language model forward pass)\\n    \\n    # Extract hidden states for action tokens\\n    last_hidden_states = language_model_output.hidden_states[-1]\\n    actions_hidden_states = last_hidden_states[\\n        :,\\n        NUM_PATCHES + NUM_PROMPT_TOKENS : NUM_PATCHES + NUM_PROMPT_TOKENS + ACTION_DIM * NUM_ACTIONS_CHUNK,\\n        :,\\n    ]\\n    \\n    # Handle different prediction methods\\n    if action_head is not None:\\n        # L1 regression prediction\\n        normalized_actions = action_head.predict_action(actions_hidden_states)\\n        normalized_actions = normalized_actions.reshape(NUM_ACTIONS_CHUNK, ACTION_DIM)\\n        normalized_actions = normalized_actions.float().cpu().detach().numpy()\\n    else:\\n        # Discrete token-based prediction\\n        predicted_action_token_ids = (\\n            language_model_output.logits[\\n                :,\\n                NUM_PATCHES + NUM_PROMPT_TOKENS : NUM_PATCHES + NUM_PROMPT_TOKENS + ACTION_DIM * NUM_ACTIONS_CHUNK,\\n            ]\\n            .argmax(dim=2)\\n            .cpu()\\n            .numpy()\\n        )\\n        # ... discrete decoding logic\\n```\\n\\n**Key flow in `predict_action`:**\\n\\n```python\\nif use_diffusion:\\n    normalized_actions, actions_hidden_states = self._run_diffusion_prediction(...)\\nelse:\\n    # If action_head is provided, uses L1 regression; otherwise uses discrete tokens\\n    normalized_actions, actions_hidden_states = self._regression_or_discrete_prediction(\\n        input_embeddings,\\n        all_actions_mask,\\n        projected_patch_embeddings,\\n        attention_mask,\\n        labels,\\n        NUM_PATCHES,\\n        NUM_PROMPT_TOKENS,\\n        action_head,  # that produces embeddings of a batch of timesteps.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n    For example, at train time, the input might be a batch of 32 randomly sampled diffusion timesteps -> shape (32,)\\\\\\\\n    Then the output would be a batch of 32 timestep embeddings -> shape (32, D)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n    Adapted from: https://github.com/real-stanford/diffusion_policy/blob/main/diffusion_policy/model/diffusion/positional_embedding.py\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n    def __init__(self, dim):\\\\\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\\\\\n        self.dim = dim  # dimensionality of the positional encoding\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n    def forward(self, x):\\\\\\\\n        # x: (batch_size,)\\\\\\\\n        device = x.device\\\\\\\\n        assert self.dim % 2 == 0, f\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"# dimensions must be even but got {self.dim}\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n        half_dim = self.dim // 2\\\\\\\\n        exponent = torch.arange(half_dim, device=device) * -math.log(10000) / (half_dim - 1)  # shape: (D/2,)\\\\\\\\n        emb = torch.exp(exponent)  # shape: (D/2,)\\\\\\\\n        emb = x[:, None] * emb[None, :]  # shape: (batch_size, 1) * (1, D/2) -> (batch_size, D/2)\\\\\\\\n        emb = torch.cat((emb.sin(), emb.cos()), dim=-1)  # shape: (batch_size, D)\\\\\\\\n        return emb\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nclass MLPResNetBlock(nn.Module):\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"One MLP ResNet block with a residual connection.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n    def __init__(self, dim):\\\\\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\\\\\n        self.dim = dim\\\\\\\\n        self.ffn = nn.Sequential(  # feedforward network, similar to the ones in Transformers\\\\\\\\n            nn.LayerNorm(dim),\\\\\\\\n            nn.Linear(dim, dim),\\\\\\\\n            nn.ReLU(),\\\\\\\\n        )\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n    def forward(self, x):\\\\\\\\n        # x: (batch_size, hidden_dim)\\\\\\\\n        # We follow the module ordering of \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Pre-Layer Normalization\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" feedforward networks in Transformers as\\\\\\\\n        # described here: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2002.04745.pdf\\\\\\\\n        identity = x\\\\\\\\n        x = self.ffn(x)\\\\\\\\n        x = x + identity\\\\\\\\n        return x\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nclass MLPResNet(nn.Module):\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"MLP with residual connection blocks.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n    def __init__(self, num_blocks, input_dim, hidden_dim, output_dim):\\\\\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\\\\\n        self.layer_norm1 = nn.LayerNorm(input_dim)\\\\\\\\n        self.fc1 = nn.Linear(input_dim, hidden_dim)\\\\\\\\n        self.relu = nn.ReLU()\\\\\\\\n        self.mlp_resnet_blocks = nn.ModuleList()\\\\\\\\n        for _ in range(num_blocks):\\\\\\\\n            self.mlp_resnet_blocks.append(MLPResNetBlock(dim=hidden_dim))\\\\\\\\n        self.layer_norm2 = nn.LayerNorm(hidden_dim)\\\\\\\\n        self.fc2 = nn.Linear(hidden_dim, output_dim)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n    def forward(self, x):\\\\\\\\n        # x: (batch_size, input_dim)\\\\\\\\n        x = self.layer_norm1(x)  # shape: (batch_size, input_dim)\\\\\\\\n        x = self.fc1(x)  # shape: (batch_size, hidden_dim)\\\\\\\\n        x = self.relu(x)  # shape: (batch_size, hidden_dim)\\\\\\\\n self.action_dim = action_dim\\\\\\\\n        self.model = MLPResNet(\\\\\\\\n            num_blocks=2, input_dim=input_dim*ACTION_DIM, hidden_dim=hidden_dim, output_dim=action_dim\\\\\\\\n shape: (batch_size, chunk_len, rearranged_hidden_dim=action_dim*hidden_dim)\\\\\\\\n        #\\\\\\\\n        # output: predicted noise\\\\\\\\n        # - shape: (batch_size, action_dim)\\\\\\\\n        output = self.mlp_resnet(obs)\\\\\\\\n        return output\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nclass DiffusionActionHead(nn.Module):\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n    Simple MLP-based action head that generates continuous actions via conditional denoising diffusion process.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n    Loosely inspired by: https://github.com/real-stanford/diffusion_policy/blob/main/diffusion_policy/model/diffusion/transformer_for_diffusion.py\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n    def __init__(\\\\\\\\n        self,\\\\\\\\n        input_dim=4096,\\\\\\\\n        hidden_dim=4096,\\\\\\\\n        action_dim=7,\\\\\\\\n        num_diffusion_steps_train=50,\\\\\\\\n    ):\\\\\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\\\\\n        self.action_dim = action_dim\\\\\\\\n        self.noise_predictor = NoisePredictionModel(\\\\\\\\n            transformer_hidden_dim=hidden_dim*ACTION_DIM, hidden_dim=hidden_dim, action_dim=action_dim\\\\\\\\n        )\\\\\\\\n        self.num_diffusion_steps_train = num_diffusion_steps_train\\\\\\\\n        self.noise_scheduler = DDIMScheduler(num_train_timesteps=num_diffusion_steps_train, beta_schedule=\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"squaredcos_cap_v2\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\")\\\\\\\\n        self.time_encoder = SinusoidalPositionalEncoding(dim=hidden_dim)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n    def sample_noisy_actions(self, ground_truth_actions):\\\\\\\\n        \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n        Samples noise and applies noise to ground-truth actions to produce noisy actions, which are\\\\\\\\n        used as input in the noise prediction network. Returns noise, noisy actions, and the\\\\\\\\n        corresponding diffusion timestep embeddings.\\\\\\\\n        \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n        # ground_truth_actions: ground-truth actions\\\\\\\\n        # - shape: (batch_size, chunk_len, action_dim)\\\\\\\\n        batch_size = ground_truth_actions.shape[0]\\\\\\\\n        device = ground_truth_actions.device\\\\\\\\n        # Sample random noise with shape equal to actions, used for closed-form forward diffusion.\\\\\\\\n        noise = torch.randn(size=(batch_size, NUM_ACTIONS_CHUNK, ACTION_DIM), device=device, dtype=ground_truth_actions.dtype)  # (B, chunk_len, action_dim)\\\\\\\\n        # Sample random diffusion timesteps (one for each action in batch).\\\\\\\\n        timesteps = torch.randint(\\\\\\\\n            low=0, high=self.noise_scheduler.config.num_train_timesteps, size=(batch_size,), device=device\\\\\\\\n        )\\\\\\\\n        # Add noise to clean actions according to the magnitude at each diffusion timestep via\\\\\\\\n        # closed-form forward diffusion.\\\\\\\\n        noisy_actions = self.noise_scheduler.add_noise(ground_truth_actions, noise, timesteps)  # (B, chunk_len, action_dim)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n        # Get diffusion timestep embeddings as well\\\\\\\\n        diffusion_timestep_embeddings = self.time_encoder(timesteps).to(noisy_actions.dtype).to(noisy_actions.device)  # (B, llm_dim)\\\\\\\\n        diffusion_timestep_embeddings = diffusion_timestep_embeddings.unsqueeze(1)  # (B, 1, llm_dim)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n        return_dict = dict(\\\\\\\\n            noise=noise,\\\\\\\\n            noisy_actions=noisy_actions,\\\\\\\\n            diffusion_timestep_embeddings=diffusion_timestep_embeddings,\\\\\\\\n        )\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n        return return_dict\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n    def predict_noise(self, observation embeddings,\\\\\\\\n        noisy action embeddings, and diffusion timestep embedding), predicts the noise applied to the actions.\\\\\\\\n (batch_size, chunk_len * action_dim, hidden_dim)\\\\\\\\n        batch_size = actions_hidden_states.shape[0]\\\\\\\\n        device = actions_hidden_states.device\\\\\\\\n        rearranged_actions_hidden_states = actions_hidden_states.reshape(batch_size, NUM_ACTIONS_CHUNK, -1)  # (batch_size, chunk_len, action_dim * hidden_dim)\\\\\\\\n        # Get diffusion model's noise prediction.\\\\\\\\n        noise_pred = self.noise_predictor(rearranged_actions_hidden_states)\\\\\\\\n        return noise_pred\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"stderr\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\", feedforward network, similar to the ones in Transformers\\\\n            nn.LayerNorm(dim),\\\\n            nn.Linear(dim, dim),\\\\n            nn.ReLU(),\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, x):\\\\n        # x: (batch_size, hidden_dim)\\\\n        # We follow the module ordering of \\\\\\\"Pre-Layer Normalization\\\\\\\" feedforward networks in Transformers as\\\\n        # described here: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2002.04745.pdf\\\\n        identity = x\\\\n        x = self.ffn(x)\\\\n        x = x + identity\\\\n        return x\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass MLPResNet(nn.Module):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"MLP with residual connection blocks.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    def __init__(self, num_blocks, input_dim, hidden_dim, output_dim):\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        self.layer_norm1 = nn.LayerNorm(input_dim)\\\\n        self.fc1 = nn.Linear(input_dim, hidden_dim)\\\\n        self.relu = nn.ReLU()\\\\n        self.mlp_resnet_blocks = nn.ModuleList()\\\\n        for _ in range(num_blocks):\\\\n            self.mlp_resnet_blocks.append(MLPResNetBlock(dim=hidden_dim))\\\\n        self.layer_norm2 = nn.LayerNorm(hidden_dim)\\\\n        self.fc2 = nn.Linear(hidden_dim, output_dim)\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, x):\\\\n        # x: (batch_size, input_dim)\\\\n        x = self.layer_norm1(x)  # shape: (batch_size, input_dim)\\\\n        x = self.fc1(x)  # shape: (batch_size, hidden_dim)\\\\n        x = self.relu(x)  # shape: (batch_size, hidden_dim)\\\\n        for block in self.mlp_resnet_blocks:\\\\n            x = block(x)  # shape: (batch_size, hidden_dim)\\\\n        x = self.layer_norm2(x)  # shape: (batch_size, hidden_dim)\\\\n        x = self.fc2(x)  # shape: (batch_size, output_dim)\\\\n        return x\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass L1RegressionActionHead(nn.Module):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Simple MLP-based action head that generates continuous actions via L1 regression.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    def __init__(\\\\n        self,\\\\n        input_dim=4096,\\\\n        hidden_dim=4096,\\\\n        action_dim=7,\\\\n    ):\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        self.action_dim = action_dim\\\\n        self.model = MLPResNet(\\\\n            num_blocks=2, input_dim=input_dim*ACTION_DIM, hidden_dim=hidden_dim, output_dim=action_dim\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n    def predict_action(self, actions_hidden_states):\\\\n        # actions_hidden_states: last hidden states of Transformer corresponding to action tokens in sequence\\\\n        # - shape: (batch_size, chunk_len * action_dim, hidden_dim)\\\\n        # \\\\n\\\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 1053 字符] ...\\\\n\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(\\\\n        self,\\\\n        obs,\\\\n    ):\\\\n        # obs: observation embeddings to condition the generation on\\\\n        # - shape: (batch_size, chunk_len, rearranged_hidden_dim=action_dim*hidden_dim)\\\\n        #\\\\n        # output: predicted noise\\\\n        # - shape: (batch_size, action_dim)\\\\n        output = self.mlp_resnet(obs)\\\\n        return output\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass DiffusionActionHead(nn.Module):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    Simple MLP-based action head that generates continuous actions via conditional denoising diffusion process.\\\\n\\\\n    Loosely inspired by: https://github.com/real-stanford/diffusion_policy/blob/main/diffusion_policy/model/diffusion/transformer_for_diffusion.py\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(\\\\n        self,\\\\n        input_dim=4096,\\\\n        hidden_dim=4096,\\\\n        action_dim=7,\\\\n        num_diffusion_steps_train=50,\\\\n    ):\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        self.action_dim = action_dim\\\\n        self.noise_predictor = NoisePredictionModel(\\\\n            transformer_hidden_dim=hidden_dim*ACTION_DIM, hidden_dim=hidden_dim, action_dim=action_dim\\\\n        )\\\\n        self.num_diffusion_steps_train = num_diffusion_steps_train\\\\n        self.noise_scheduler = DDIMScheduler(num_train_timesteps=num_diffusion_steps_train, beta_schedule=\\\\\\\"squaredcos_cap_v2\\\\\\\")\\\\n        self.time_encoder = SinusoidalPositionalEncoding(dim=hidden_dim)\\\\n\\\\n    def sample_noisy_actions(self, ground_truth_actions):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        Samples noise and applies noise to ground-truth actions to produce noisy actions, which are\\\\n        used as input in the noise prediction network. Returns noise, noisy actions, and the\\\\n        corresponding diffusion timestep embeddings.\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        # ground_truth_actions: ground-truth actions\\\\n        # - shape: (batch_size, chunk_len, action_dim)\\\\n        batch_size = ground_truth_actions.shape[0]\\\\n        device = ground_truth_actions.device\\\\n        # Sample random noise with shape equal to actions, used for closed-form forward diffusion.\\\\n        noise = torch.randn(size=(batch_size, NUM_ACTIONS_CHUNK, ACTION_DIM), device=device, dtype=ground_truth_actions.dtype)  # (B, chunk_len, action_dim)\\\\n        # Sample random diffusion timesteps (one for each action in batch).\\\\n        timesteps = torch.randint(\\\\n            low=0, high=self.noise_scheduler.config.num_train_timesteps, size=(batch_size,), device=device\\\\n        )\\\\n        # Add noise to clean actions according to the magnitude at each diffusion timestep via\\\\n        # closed-form forward diffusion.\\\\n        noisy_actions = self.noise_scheduler.add_noise(ground_truth_actions, noise, timesteps)  # (B, chunk_len, action_dim)\\\\n\\\\n        # Get diffusion timestep embeddings as well\\\\n        diffusion_timestep_embeddings = self.time_encoder(timesteps).to(noisy_actions.dtype).to(noisy_actions.device)  # (B, llm_dim)\\\\n        diffusion_timestep_embeddings = diffusion_timestep_embeddings.unsqueeze(1)  # (B, 1, llm_dim)\\\\n\\\\n        return_dict = dict(\\\\n            noise=noise,\\\\n            noisy_actions=noisy_actions,\\\\n            diffusion_timestep_embeddings=diffusion_timestep_embeddings,\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n        return return_dict\\\\n\\\\n    def predict_noise(self, actions_hidden_states):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        Given a batch of last hidden Transformer layer embeddings (which fuse the vision-language observation embeddings,\\\\n        noisy action embeddings, and diffusion timestep embedding), predicts the noise applied to the actions.\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        # actions_hidden_states: last hidden states of Transformer corresponding to action tokens in sequence\\\\n        # - shape: (batch_size, chunk_len * action_dim, hidden_dim)\\\\n        batch_size = actions_hidden_states.shape[0]\\\\n        device = actions_hidden_states.device\\\\n        rearranged_actions_hidden_states = actions_hidden_states.reshape(batch_size, NUM_ACTIONS_CHUNK, -1)  # (batch_size, chunk_len, action_dim * hidden_dim)\\\\n        # Get diffusion model's noise prediction.\\\\n        noise_pred = self.noise_predictor(rearranged_actions_hidden_states)\\\\n        return noise_pred\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.44}}\"}}}\n{\"type\":\"user\",\"message\":{\"role\":\"user\",\"content\":[{\"tool_use_id\":\"toolu_019B12EBgGB4oqceDjoE5ryi\",\"type\":\"tool_result\",\"content\":\"{\\\"result\\\":\\\"{\\\\\\\"success\\\\\\\": true, \\\\\\\"data\\\\\\\": {\\\\\\\"stdout\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Implementation of additional projectors for additional inputs to the VLA models.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\nimport torch\\\\\\\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nclass ProprioProjector(nn.Module):\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n    Projects proprio state inputs into the LLM's embedding space.\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n    def __init__(self, llm_dim: int, proprio_dim: int) -> None:\\\\\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\\\\\n        self.llm_dim = llm_dim\\\\\\\\n        self.proprio_dim = proprio_dim\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n        self.fc1 = nn.Linear(self.proprio_dim, self.llm_dim, bias=True)\\\\\\\\n        self.fc2 = nn.Linear(self.llm_dim, self.llm_dim, bias=True)\\\\\\\\n        self.act_fn1 = nn.GELU()\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n    def forward(self, proprio: torch.Tensor = None) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\\\\\n        # [截断: 输出过长，省略 22203 字符] ...\\\\n\\\\nE)\\\\n\\\\n        # Convert to PIL image\\\\n        pil_image = Image.fromarray(image).convert(\\\\\\\"RGB\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n        # Apply center crop if configured\\\\n        if cfg.center_crop:\\\\n            pil_image = center_crop_image(pil_image)\\\\n\\\\n        processed_images.append(pil_image)\\\\n\\\\n    return processed_images\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef get_vla_action(\\\\n    cfg: Any,\\\\n    vla: torch.nn.Module,\\\\n    processor: Any,\\\\n    obs: Dict[str, Any],\\\\n    task_label: str,\\\\n    action_head: Optional[torch.nn.Module] = None,\\\\n    proprio_projector: Optional[torch.nn.Module] = None,\\\\n    noisy_action_projector: Optional[torch.nn.Module] = None,\\\\n    use_film: bool = False,\\\\n) -> List[np.ndarray]:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    Generate action predictions with the VLA policy.\\\\n\\\\n    Args:\\\\n        cfg: Configuration object with parameters\\\\n        vla: The VLA model\\\\n        processor: Model processor for inputs\\\\n        obs: Observation dictionary\\\\n        task_label: Text description of the task\\\\n        action_head: Optional action head for continuous actions\\\\n        proprio_projector: Optional proprioception projector\\\\n        noisy_action_projector: Optional noisy action projector for diffusion\\\\n        use_film: Whether to use FiLM\\\\n\\\\n    Returns:\\\\n        List[np.ndarray]: Predicted actions\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    with torch.inference_mode():\\\\n\\\\n        # Collect all input images\\\\n        all_images = [obs[\\\\\\\"full_image\\\\\\\"]]\\\\n        if cfg.num_images_in_input > 1:\\\\n            all_images.extend([obs[k] for k in obs.keys() if \\\\\\\"wrist\\\\\\\" in k])\\\\n\\\\n        # Process images\\\\n        all_images = prepare_images_for_vla(all_images, cfg)\\\\n\\\\n        # Extract primary image and additional images\\\\n        primary_image = all_images.pop(0)\\\\n\\\\n        # Build VLA prompt\\\\n        prompt = f\\\\\\\"In: What action should the robot take to {task_label.lower()}?\\\\\\\\nOut:\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n        # Process primary image\\\\n        inputs = processor(prompt, primary_image).to(DEVICE, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\\\\n\\\\n        # Process additional wrist images if any\\\\n        if all_images:\\\\n            all_wrist_inputs = [\\\\n                processor(prompt, image_wrist).to(DEVICE, dtype=torch.bfloat16) for image_wrist in all_images\\\\n            ]\\\\n            # Concatenate all images\\\\n            primary_pixel_values = inputs[\\\\\\\"pixel_values\\\\\\\"]\\\\n            all_wrist_pixel_values = [wrist_inputs[\\\\\\\"pixel_values\\\\\\\"] for wrist_inputs in all_wrist_inputs]\\\\n            inputs[\\\\\\\"pixel_values\\\\\\\"] = torch.cat([primary_pixel_values] + all_wrist_pixel_values, dim=1)\\\\n\\\\n        # Process proprioception data if used\\\\n        proprio = None\\\\n        if cfg.use_proprio:\\\\n            proprio = obs[\\\\\\\"state\\\\\\\"]\\\\n            proprio_norm_stats = vla.norm_stats[cfg.unnorm_key][\\\\\\\"proprio\\\\\\\"]\\\\n            obs[\\\\\\\"state\\\\\\\"] = normalize_proprio(proprio, proprio_norm_stats)\\\\n            proprio = obs[\\\\\\\"state\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n        # Generate action\\\\n        if action_head is None:\\\\n            # Standard VLA output (single-image inputs, discrete actions)\\\\n            action, _ = vla.predict_action(**inputs, unnorm_key=cfg.unnorm_key, do_sample=False)\\\\n        else:\\\\n            # Custom action head for continuous actions\\\\n            action, _ = vla.predict_action(\\\\n                **inputs,\\\\n                unnorm_key=cfg.unnorm_key,\\\\n                do_sample=False,\\\\n                proprio=proprio,\\\\n                proprio_projector=proprio_projector,\\\\n                noisy_action_projector=noisy_action_projector,\\\\n                action_head=action_head,\\\\n                use_film=use_film,\\\\n            )\\\\n\\\\n    # Return action chunk as list of actions\\\\n    return [action[i] for i in range(len(action))]\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef get_action_from_server(\\\\n    observation: Dict[str, Any], server_endpoint: str = \\\\\\\"http://0.0.0.0:8777/act\\\\\\\"\\\\n) -> Dict[str, Any]:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    Get VLA action from remote inference server.\\\\n\\\\n    Args:\\\\n        observation: Observation data to send to server\\\\n        server_endpoint: URL of the inference server\\\\n\\\\n    Returns:\\\\n        Dict[str, Any]: Action response from server\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    response = requests.post(\\\\n        server_endpoint,\\\\n        json=observation,\\\\n    )\\\\n    return response.json()\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.51}}\"}}}\n{\"type\":\"assistant\",\"message\":{\"model\":\"claude-opus-4-6\",\"id\":\"msg_01ArMPd1uxdv5PcsnULXqJZM\",\"type\":\"message\",\"role\":\"assistant\",\"content\":[{\"type\":\"thinking\",\"thinking\":\"Now I have a complete understanding. Returns:\\\\\\\\n        np.array: (ax,ay,az) axis-angle exponential coordinates\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n    # clip quaternion\\\\\\\\n    if quat[3] >
